import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

// Scene setup
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xffffff); // White background

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(0, 0, 5);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Controls
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.enablePan = false;
controls.minDistance = 3;
controls.maxDistance = 10;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;

// Create cube as 6 separate face meshes
const faceSize = 2;
const halfSize = faceSize / 2;
const faces = [];

// Face data: [position, rotation, normal]
const faceData = [
    // Front (+Z)
    { pos: [0, 0, halfSize], rot: [0, 0, 0], normal: [0, 0, 1] },
    // Back (-Z)
    { pos: [0, 0, -halfSize], rot: [0, Math.PI, 0], normal: [0, 0, -1] },
    // Right (+X)
    { pos: [halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, -Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [1, 0, 0] },
    // Left (-X)
    { pos: [-halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [-1, 0, 0] },
    // Top (+Y)
    { pos: [0, halfSize, 0], rot: [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, 1, 0] },
    // Bottom (-Y)
    { pos: [0, -halfSize, 0], rot: [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, -1, 0] },
];

const faceGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(faceSize, faceSize);

// Crack texture generator - produces visible (map) and alpha (alphaMap) textures
class CrackTexture {
    constructor(size = 512) {
        this.size = size;
        
        // Visible texture: black glass background with bright refraction cracks
        this.visibleCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.visibleCanvas.width = size;
        this.visibleCanvas.height = size;
        this.visibleCtx = this.visibleCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.visibleTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.visibleCanvas);
        this.visibleTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.visibleTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.visibleTexture.anisotropy = 4;
        
        // Alpha texture: white background with black cracks (for shatter pieces)
        this.alphaCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.alphaCanvas.width = size;
        this.alphaCanvas.height = size;
        this.alphaCtx = this.alphaCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.alphaTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.alphaCanvas);
        this.alphaTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.alphaTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        
        this.cracks = []; // Array of crack paths
        this.impactPoints = []; // Impact points for radial cracks
        this.clear();
    }

    clear() {
        // Visible: deep black glass base
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        
        // Alpha: white background (opaque)
        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        
        this.cracks = [];
        this.impactPoints = [];
        this.updateTextures();
    }

    // Add impact point for radial crack pattern
    addImpact(x, y) {
        // Convert to texture coordinates (0-1)
        const tx = (x / this.size) * this.size;
        const ty = (y / this.size) * this.size;
        this.impactPoints.push({ x: tx, y: ty });
    }

    // Generate a lightning-like crack path from impact points
    generateCrack() {
        const path = [];
        
        // Start from a random impact point or random position
        let startX, startY;
        if (this.impactPoints.length > 0 && Math.random() < 0.7) {
            const impact = this.impactPoints[Math.floor(Math.random() * this.impactPoints.length)];
            startX = impact.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
            startY = impact.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
        } else {
            startX = Math.random() * this.size;
            startY = Math.random() * this.size;
        }
        path.push({ x: startX, y: startY });

        const segments = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
        let x = startX;
        let y = startY;
        let angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
            const length = 15 + Math.random() * 35;
            angle += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5; // More jagged
            x += Math.cos(angle) * length;
            y += Math.sin(angle) * length;

            // Bounce off edges with energy loss
            if (x < 0) { x = Math.abs(x); angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (x > this.size) { x = 2 * this.size - x; angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (y < 0) { y = Math.abs(y); angle = -angle; }
            if (y > this.size) { y = 2 * this.size - y; angle = -angle; }

            path.push({ x, y });

            // Branch more aggressively
            if (Math.random() < 0.25 && i > 1) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() - 0.5) * Math.PI * 0.8;
                const branchLength = 10 + Math.random() * 25;
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
            
            // Secondary micro-branches
            if (Math.random() < 0.15 && i > 2) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (Math.PI/2 + Math.random() * Math.PI/4);
                const branchLength = 5 + Math.random() * 15;
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
        }
        this.cracks.push(path);
    }

    // Add N new cracks and redraw
    addCracks(count, impactX, impactY) {
        if (impactX !== undefined && impactY !== undefined) {
            this.addImpact(impactX, impactY);
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            this.generateCrack();
        }
        this.redraw();
    }

    redraw() {
        // Visible texture: black glass base
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        // Alpha texture: white bg, black cracks
        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        // Draw impact craters first (subtle)
        this.impactPoints.forEach(pt => {
            // Visible: subtle crater
            const grad = this.visibleCtx.createRadialGradient(pt.x, pt.y, 0, pt.x, pt.y, 8);
            grad.addColorStop(0, '#1a1a1a');
            grad.addColorStop(1, '#000000');
            this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = grad;
            this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
            this.visibleCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.visibleCtx.fill();
            
            // Alpha: small hole
            this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
            this.alphaCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.alphaCtx.fill();
        });

        // Draw main cracks with glass-like refraction effect
        this.cracks.forEach((path, pathIndex) => {
            if (path.length < 2) return;
            
            // Calculate line width based on crack hierarchy (main vs branch)
            const isMain = pathIndex < this.cracks.length * 0.3;
            const baseWidth = isMain ? 2.5 : 1.2;
            
            // ===== VISIBLE TEXTURE: Glass refraction cracks =====
            // 1. Dark core (deep crack)
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#0a0a0a';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth + 1;
            this.visibleCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.visibleCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.visibleCtx.shadowColor = '#000000';
            this.visibleCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            
            // 2. Bright refraction edge (light catching crack walls)
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.4;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            // 3. Subsurface scattering glow (glass internal reflection)
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#444444';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 1.8;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
            this.visibleCtx.filter = 'blur(1px)';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.filter = 'none';
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            // 4. Fine highlight line
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#cccccc';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.15;
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);

            // ===== ALPHA TEXTURE: Transparent cracks for shatter =====
            this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth + 1.5; // Wider for clean transparency
            this.alphaCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.alphaCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.drawPath(this.alphaCtx, path);
        });

        // Add fine detail cracks (micro-fractures)
        this.cracks.forEach(path => {
            if (path.length < 3) return;
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length - 1; i += 3) {
                if (Math.random() < 0.4) {
                    const px = path[i].x;
                    const py = path[i].y;
                    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                    const len = 3 + Math.random() * 12;
                    
                    // Visible micro-crack
                    this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#888888';
                    this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = 0.6;
                    this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                    this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
                    this.visibleCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.visibleCtx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.visibleCtx.stroke();
                    this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                    
                    // Alpha micro-crack
                    this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
                    this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = 1.0;
                    this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
                    this.alphaCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.alphaCtx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.alphaCtx.stroke();
                }
            }
        });

        this.updateTextures();
    }

    drawPath(ctx, path) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(path[0].x, path[0].y);
        for (let i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
            // Slight curve for organic feel
            const xc = (path[i-1].x + path[i].x) / 2;
            const yc = (path[i-1].y + path[i].y) / 2;
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(path[i-1].x, path[i-1].y, xc, yc);
        }
        // Last segment
        if (path.length > 1) {
            const last = path[path.length - 1];
            const prev = path[path.length - 2];
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(prev.x, prev.y, last.x, last.y);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
    }

    updateTextures() {
        this.visibleTexture.needsUpdate = true;
        this.alphaTexture.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    getVisibleTexture() {
        return this.visibleTexture;
    }

    getAlphaTexture() {
        return this.alphaTexture;
    }

    getCrackCount() {
        return this.cracks.length;
    }
}

faceData.forEach((data, index) => {
    // Each face gets its own crack texture generator
    const crackTexture = new CrackTexture(512);
    
    // Main face material: black glass with crack map
    const faceMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x000000,
        roughness: 0.1,        // Very smooth glass
        metalness: 0.0,        // Dielectric
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(),
        envMapIntensity: 1.0,
        clearcoat: 1.0,        // Glass clearcoat
        clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
        transmission: 0.9,     // Nearly transparent glass
        thickness: 0.5,
        ior: 1.5,              // Glass IOR
    });
    
    const face = new THREE.Mesh(faceGeometry, faceMaterial);
    face.position.set(...data.pos);
    face.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    face.userData = { 
        faceIndex: index, 
        originalPosition: data.pos, 
        originalRotation: data.rot,
        clickCount: 0,
        maxClicks: 4,
        crackTexture: crackTexture,
    };
    scene.add(face);
    faces.push(face);
});

// Wireframe - create edges for the whole cube
const cubeGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(faceSize, faceSize, faceSize);
const edges = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(cubeGeometry);
const lineMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ 
    color: 0x333333, 
    transparent: true, 
    opacity: 0.4 
});
const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, lineMaterial);
scene.add(wireframe);

// Group for rotation (contains faces + wireframe)
const cubeGroup = new THREE.Group();
faces.forEach(face => cubeGroup.add(face));
cubeGroup.add(wireframe);
scene.add(cubeGroup);

// Lighting - multiple soft white lights for glass reflections
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.6);
scene.add(ambientLight);

const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.0);
directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
scene.add(directionalLight);

const fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.6);
fillLight.position.set(-5, 5, -7);
scene.add(fillLight);

const topLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
topLight.position.set(0, 10, 0);
scene.add(topLight);

const sideLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
sideLight1.position.set(10, 0, 5);
scene.add(sideLight1);

const sideLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
sideLight2.position.set(-10, 0, -5);
scene.add(sideLight2);

// Add a point light for sparkle
const sparkleLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffffff, 0.3, 10);
sparkleLight.position.set(0, 5, 5);
scene.add(sparkleLight);

// Raycaster for click detection
const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

// Click handler
function onClick(event) {
    mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
    mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

    raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
    const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(faces.filter(f => f.visible), true);

    if (intersects.length > 0) {
        const hit = intersects[0];
        const hitFace = hit.object;
        const uv = hit.uv;
        
        // Convert UV to texture coordinates
        const texX = uv.x * 512;
        const texY = (1 - uv.y) * 512; // Flip Y for canvas
        
        handleFaceClick(hitFace, texX, texY);
    }
}

function handleFaceClick(face, texX, texY) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    userData.clickCount++;
    
    // Add cracks radiating from impact point
    // Click 1: 3 cracks, Click 2: 6 cracks, Click 3: 10 cracks, Click 4: shatter
    const cracksToAdd = [3, 6, 10, 0][userData.clickCount - 1] || 0;
    if (cracksToAdd > 0) {
        userData.crackTexture.addCracks(cracksToAdd, texX, texY);
    }
    
    // Visual impact flash
    createImpactFlash(face, texX, texY);
    
    // If 4th click, animate shatter disappearance
    if (userData.clickCount >= userData.maxClicks) {
        animateFaceShatter(face);
    }
}

function createImpactFlash(face, texX, texY) {
    // Brief white flash at impact point
    const flashGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(0.01, 0.15, 32);
    const flashMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: 0xffffff,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.8,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        depthWrite: false,
    });
    const flash = new THREE.Mesh(flashGeo, flashMat);
    
    // Position at face with slight offset
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldNormal = new THREE.Vector3();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldDirection(worldNormal);
    flash.position.copy(worldPos).addScaledVector(worldNormal, 0.011);
    flash.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), worldNormal);
    
    scene.add(flash);
    
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 150;
    
    function animateFlash() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
        
        flash.material.opacity = 0.8 * (1 - eased);
        flash.scale.setScalar(1 + eased * 3);
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animateFlash);
        } else {
            scene.remove(flash);
            flash.geometry.dispose();
            flash.material.dispose();
        }
    }
    animateFlash();
}

function animateFaceShatter(face) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    const crackTexture = userData.crackTexture;
    
    // Create shatter pieces from the crack pattern
    const pieces = createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture);
    
    // Animate pieces flying off
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 800;
    
    function animatePieces() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = Math.pow(progress, 2.5); // easeInQuint
        
        pieces.forEach((piece, i) => {
            const delay = i * 0.015;
            const pieceProgress = Math.max(0, (progress - delay) / (1 - delay));
            if (pieceProgress <= 0) return;
            
            const pieceEased = Math.pow(pieceProgress, 2);
            
            // Fly outward and rotate
            piece.position.x += piece.userData.velocity.x * pieceEased * 0.8;
            piece.position.y += piece.userData.velocity.y * pieceEased * 0.8;
            piece.position.z += piece.userData.velocity.z * pieceEased * 0.8;
            
            piece.rotation.x += piece.userData.angularVelocity.x * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.y += piece.userData.angularVelocity.y * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.z += piece.userData.angularVelocity.z * pieceEased;
            
            // Fade out with sparkle
            piece.material.opacity = 1 - pieceEased;
            piece.material.transparent = true;
            piece.material.needsUpdate = true;
        });
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animatePieces);
        } else {
            // Cleanup
            pieces.forEach(piece => {
                scene.remove(piece);
                piece.geometry.dispose();
                piece.material.dispose();
            });
            face.visible = false;
            // Reset crack texture for potential reuse
            crackTexture.clear();
            userData.clickCount = 0;
        }
    }
    
    // Hide original face immediately
    face.visible = false;
    animatePieces();
}

function createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture) {
    const pieces = [];
    const pieceCount = 15 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
    
    // Get face world position and rotation
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldQuaternion(worldQuat);
    
    for (let i = 0; i < pieceCount; i++) {
        // Create irregular piece geometry
        const size = 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.18;
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size, size, 0.03);
        
        // Shatter piece material: glass with cracks
        const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x000000,
            roughness: 0.1,
            metalness: 0.0,
            side: THREE.DoubleSide,
            map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(),
            alphaMap: crackTexture.getAlphaTexture(),
            transparent: true,
            alphaTest: 0.01,
            clearcoat: 1.0,
            clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
            transmission: 0.9,
            thickness: 0.3,
            ior: 1.5,
        });
        mat.needsUpdate = true;
        
        const piece = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        
        // Position near face center with offset
        const offset = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1
        );
        offset.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        piece.position.copy(worldPos).add(offset);
        piece.quaternion.copy(worldQuat);
        
        // Random velocity outward from face center
        const dir = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
            -0.2 - Math.random() * 0.3
        ).normalize();
        const speed = 0.015 + Math.random() * 0.025;
        const velocity = dir.multiplyScalar(speed);
        velocity.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        
        // Random angular velocity
        const angularVelocity = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08
        );
        
        piece.userData = { velocity, angularVelocity };
        scene.add(piece);
        pieces.push(piece);
    }
    
    return pieces;
}

window.addEventListener('click', onClick);

// Minimal Audio Engine
class AudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.15;
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    }

    start() {
        if (this.isPlaying) return;
        this.isPlaying = true;
        this.scheduleLoop();
    }

    stop() {
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    scheduleLoop() {
        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81];
        frequencies.forEach((freq) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

// Resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// Animation loop
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    
    controls.update();
    
    // Subtle cube rotation
    cubeGroup.rotation.x += 0.0005;
    cubeGroup.rotation.y += 0.0008;
    
    // Pulse wireframe opacity slightly
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    wireframe.material.opacity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.1;
    
    // Animate sparkle light
    sparkleLight.position.x = Math.sin(time * 0.7) * 3;
    sparkleLight.position.z = Math.cos(time * 0.5) * 3 + 5;
    
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

animate();

The user wants the wireframe edges to disappear along with the face when it shatters. Currently, the wireframe is a single LineSegments object for the entire cube. I need to break it down into separate edge pieces per face, or at least track which edges belong to which face and hide them when the face shatters.

Let me think about the cube edges:
- A cube has 12 edges
- Each face has 4 edges
- Each edge is shared by 2 faces

So when a face disappears, I should hide its 4 edges, but only if the adjacent face is also gone (or just hide them regardless since the face is gone).

Actually, the simplest approach: create separate wireframe pieces for each face (4 edges per face), and when a face shatters, hide its corresponding wireframe piece.

// Crack texture generator - produces visible (map) and alpha (alphaMap) textures
class CrackTexture {
    constructor(size = 512) {
        this.size = size;
        
        // Visible texture: black glass background with bright refraction cracks
        this.visibleCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.visibleCanvas.width = size;
        this.visibleCanvas.height = size;
        this.visibleCtx = this.visibleCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.visibleTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.visibleCanvas);
        this.visibleTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.visibleTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.visibleTexture.anisotropy = 4;
        
        // Alpha texture: white background with black cracks (for shatter pieces)
        this.alphaCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.alphaCanvas.width = size;
        this.alphaCanvas.height = size;
        this.alphaCtx = this.alphaCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.alphaTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.alphaCanvas);
        this.alphaTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.alphaTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        
        this.cracks = []; // Array of crack paths
        this.impactPoints = []; // Impact points for radial cracks
        this.clear();
    }

    clear() {
        // Visible: deep black glass base
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        
        // Alpha: white background (opaque)
        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        
        this.cracks = [];
        this.impactPoints = [];
        this.updateTextures();
    }

    // Add impact point for radial crack pattern
    addImpact(x, y) {
        const tx = (x / this.size) * this.size;
        const ty = (y / this.size) * this.size;
        this.impactPoints.push({ x: tx, y: ty });
    }

    // Generate a lightning-like crack path from impact points
    generateCrack() {
        const path = [];
        
        let startX, startY;
        if (this.impactPoints.length > 0 && Math.random() < 0.7) {
            const impact = this.impactPoints[Math.floor(Math.random() * this.impactPoints.length)];
            startX = impact.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
            startY = impact.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
        } else {
            startX = Math.random() * this.size;
            startY = Math.random() * this.size;
        }
        path.push({ x: startX, y: startY });

        const segments = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
        let x = startX;
        let y = startY;
        let angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
            const length = 15 + Math.random() * 35;
            angle += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5;
            x += Math.cos(angle) * length;
            y += Math.sin(angle) * length;

            if (x < 0) { x = Math.abs(x); angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (x > this.size) { x = 2 * this.size - x; angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (y < 0) { y = Math.abs(y); angle = -angle; }
            if (y > this.size) { y = 2 * this.size - y; angle = -angle; }

            path.push({ x, y });

            if (Math.random() < 0.25 && i > 1) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() - 0.5) * Math.PI * 0.8;
                const branchLength = 10 + Math.random() * 25;
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
            
            if (Math.random() < 0.15 && i > 2) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (Math.PI/2 + Math.random() * Math.PI/4);
                const branchLength = 5 + Math.random() * 15;
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
        }
        this.cracks.push(path);
    }

    addCracks(count, impactX, impactY) {
        if (impactX !== undefined && impactY !== undefined) {
            this.addImpact(impactX, impactY);
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            this.generateCrack();
        }
        this.redraw();
    }

    redraw() {
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        this.impactPoints.forEach(pt => {
            const grad = this.visibleCtx.createRadialGradient(pt.x, pt.y, 0, pt.x, pt.y, 8);
            grad.addColorStop(0, '#1a1a1a');
            grad.addColorStop(1, '#000000');
            this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = grad;
            this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
            this.visibleCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.visibleCtx.fill();
            
            this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
            this.alphaCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.alphaCtx.fill();
        });

        this.cracks.forEach((path, pathIndex) => {
            if (path.length < 2) return;
            
            const isMain = pathIndex < this.cracks.length * 0.3;
            const baseWidth = isMain ? 2.5 : 1.2;
            
            // VISIBLE TEXTURE: Glass refraction cracks
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#0a0a0a';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth + 1;
            this.visibleCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.visibleCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.4;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#444444';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 1.8;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
            this.visibleCtx.filter = 'blur(1px)';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.filter = 'none';
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#cccccc';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.15;
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);

            // ALPHA TEXTURE
            this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth + 1.5;
            this.alphaCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.alphaCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.drawPath(this.alphaCtx, path);
        });

        this.cracks.forEach(path => {
            if (path.length < 3) return;
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length - 1; i += 3) {
                if (Math.random() < 0.4) {
                    const px = path[i].x;
                    const py = path[i].y;
                    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                    const len = 3 + Math.random() * 12;
                    
                    this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#888888';
                    this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = 0.6;
                    this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                    this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
                    this.visibleCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.visibleCtx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.visibleCtx.stroke();
                    this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                    
                    this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
                    this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = 1.0;
                    this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
                    this.alphaCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.alphaCtx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.alphaCtx.stroke();
                }
            }
        });

        this.updateTextures();
    }

    drawPath(ctx, path) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(path[0].x, path[0].y);
        for (let i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
            const xc = (path[i-1].x + path[i].x) / 2;
            const yc = (path[i-1].y + path[i].y) / 2;
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(path[i-1].x, path[i-1].y, xc, yc);
        }
        if (path.length > 1) {
            const last = path[path.length - 1];
            const prev = path[path.length - 2];
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(prev.x, prev.y, last.x, last.y);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
    }

    updateTextures() {
        this.visibleTexture.needsUpdate = true;
        this.alphaTexture.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    getVisibleTexture() {
        return this.visibleTexture;
    }

    getAlphaTexture() {
        return this.alphaTexture;
    }
}

faceData.forEach((data, index) => {
    const crackTexture = new CrackTexture(512);
    
    const faceMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x000000,
        roughness: 0.1,
        metalness: 0.0,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(),

    });

    face.position.set(...data.pos);
    face.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    face.userData = { 
        faceIndex: index, 
        originalPosition: data.pos, 
        originalRotation: data.rot,
        clickCount: 0,
        maxClicks: 4,
        crackTexture: crackTexture,
    };
    scene.add(face);
    faces.push(face);

    // Create wireframe for this face only
    const edgeGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    const edgePositions = new Float32Array(data.edges.flat());
    edgeGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(edgePositions, 3));
    
    const edgeMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ 
        color: 0x333333, 
        transparent: true, 
        opacity: 0.4 
    });
    
    const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgeGeometry, edgeMaterial);
    wireframe.position.set(...data.pos);
    wireframe.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    scene.add(wireframe);
    faceWireframes.push(wireframe);
});

// Group for rotation (contains faces + wireframes)
const cubeGroup = new THREE.Group();
faces.forEach(face => cubeGroup.add(face));
faceWireframes.forEach(wf => cubeGroup.add(wf));
scene.add(cubeGroup);

// Lighting
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.6);

sideLight2.position.set(-10, 0, -5);
scene.add(sideLight2);

const sparkleLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffffff, 0.3, 10);
sparkleLight.position.set(0, 5, 5);
scene.add(sparkleLight);

// Raycaster
const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

function onClick(event) {
    mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
    mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

    raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
    const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(faces.filter(f => f.visible), true);

    if (intersects.length > 0) {
        const hit = intersects[0];
        const hitFace = hit.object;
        const uv = hit.uv;
        
        const texX = uv.x * 512;
        const texY = (1 - uv.y) * 512;
        
        handleFaceClick(hitFace, texX, texY);
    }
}

function handleFaceClick(face, texX, texY) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    userData.clickCount++;
    
    const cracksToAdd = [3, 6, 10, 0][userData.clickCount - 1] || 0;
    if (cracksToAdd > 0) {
        userData.crackTexture.addCracks(cracksToAdd, texX, texY);
    }
    
    createImpactFlash(face, texX, texY);
    
    if (userData.clickCount >= userData.maxClicks) {
        animateFaceShatter(face);
    }
}

function createImpactFlash(face, texX, texY) {
    const flashGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(0.01, 0.15, 32);
    const flashMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: 0xffffff,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.8,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        depthWrite: false,
    });
    const flash = new THREE.Mesh(flashGeo, flashMat);
    
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldNormal = new THREE.Vector3();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldDirection(worldNormal);
    flash.position.copy(worldPos).addScaledVector(worldNormal, 0.011);
    flash.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), worldNormal);
    
    scene.add(flash);
    
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 150;
    
    function animateFlash() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
        
        flash.material.opacity = 0.8 * (1 - eased);
        flash.scale.setScalar(1 + eased * 3);
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animateFlash);
        } else {
            scene.remove(flash);
            flash.geometry.dispose();
            flash.material.dispose();
        }
    }
    animateFlash();
}

function animateFaceShatter(face) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    const crackTexture = userData.crackTexture;
    const faceIndex = userData.faceIndex;
    
    // Hide the corresponding wireframe immediately
    if (faceWireframes[faceIndex]) {
        faceWireframes[faceIndex].visible = false;
    }
    
    const pieces = createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture);
    
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 800;
    
    function animatePieces() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = Math.pow(progress, 2.5);
        
        pieces.forEach((piece, i) => {
            const delay = i * 0.015;
            const pieceProgress = Math.max(0, (progress - delay) / (1 - delay));
            if (pieceProgress <= 0) return;
            
            const pieceEased = Math.pow(pieceProgress, 2);
            
            piece.position.x += piece.userData.velocity.x * pieceEased * 0.8;
            piece.position.y += piece.userData.velocity.y * pieceEased * 0.8;
            piece.position.z += piece.userData.velocity.z * pieceEased * 0.8;
            
            piece.rotation.x += piece.userData.angularVelocity.x * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.y += piece.userData.angularVelocity.y * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.z += piece.userData.angularVelocity.z * pieceEased;
            
            piece.material.opacity = 1 - pieceEased;
            piece.material.transparent = true;
            piece.material.needsUpdate = true;
        });
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animatePieces);
        } else {
            pieces.forEach(piece => {
                scene.remove(piece);
                piece.geometry.dispose();
                piece.material.dispose();
            });
            face.visible = false;
            crackTexture.clear();
            userData.clickCount = 0;
        }
    }
    
    face.visible = false;
    animatePieces();
}

function createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture) {
    const pieces = [];
    const pieceCount = 15 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
    
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldQuaternion(worldQuat);
    
    for (let i = 0; i < pieceCount; i++) {
        const size = 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.18;
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size, size, 0.03);
        
        const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x000000,
            roughness: 0.1,
            metalness: 0.0,
            side: THREE.DoubleSide,
            map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(),
            alphaMap: crackTexture.getAlphaTexture(),
            transparent: true,
            alphaTest: 0.01,
            clearcoat: 1.0,
            clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
            transmission: 0.9,
            thickness: 0.3,
            ior: 1.5,
        });
        mat.needsUpdate = true;
        
        const piece = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        
        const offset = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1
        );
        offset.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        piece.position.copy(worldPos).add(offset);
        piece.quaternion.copy(worldQuat);
        
        const dir = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
            -0.2 - Math.random() * 0.3
        ).normalize();
        const speed = 0.015 + Math.random() * 0.025;
        const velocity = dir.multiplyScalar(speed);
        velocity.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        
        const angularVelocity = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08
        );
        
        piece.userData = { velocity, angularVelocity };
        scene.add(piece);
        pieces.push(piece);
    }
    
    return pieces;
}

window.addEventListener('click', onClick);

// Minimal Audio Engine
class AudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.15;
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    }

    start() {
        if (this.isPlaying) return;
        this.isPlaying = true;
        this.scheduleLoop();
    }

    stop() {
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    scheduleLoop() {
        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81];
        frequencies.forEach((freq) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

// Resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// Animation loop
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    
    controls.update();
    
    cubeGroup.rotation.x += 0.0005;
    cubeGroup.rotation.y += 0.0008;
    
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    faceWireframes.forEach(wf => {
        wf.material.opacity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.1;
    });
    
    sparkleLight.position.x = Math.sin(time * 0.7) * 3;
    sparkleLight.position.z = Math.cos(time * 0.5) * 3 + 5;
    
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

animate();

#### Рёбра и грани, они должны совпадать. Сейчас ребра где-то за пределами куба, грани составляют куб. Но ребра грани не должны быть где-то отличны от грани. Нет. Ребра это просто края грани. Они не должны быть за пределами куба, за пределами грани. Они огромные украины.

The user is saying that the wireframes (edges) are not aligned with the faces - they're outside the cube, too big. The edges should exactly match the face boundaries. Let me look at the code.

The issue is in the faceData - the edges are defined with coordinates that are in world space, but then they're being positioned and rotated again. Let me check:

For the front face (+Z):
- pos: [0, 0, halfSize] (halfSize = 1)
- edges are defined with coordinates like [-halfSize, -halfSize, halfSize] which is [-1, -1, 1]

But then the wireframe is created with these positions AND then positioned at data.pos and rotated by data.rot. So the vertices are being doubled - once in the edge definition, once in the position/rotation.

The fix: The edge vertices should be defined in LOCAL space (relative to the face center), not world space. Since each face is a PlaneGeometry of size 2x2 centered at origin, the local coordinates for the edges should be:
- For a face at Z=0 in local space: corners at (-1,-1,0), (1,-1,0), (1,1,0), (-1,1,0)

But wait - the faces are created as PlaneGeometry(2,2) which by default is centered at (0,0,0) in the XY plane. Then they're positioned and rotated to form the cube.

So for the wireframe, I should create the edges in the face's LOCAL coordinate system (before rotation/positioning), which would be the four edges of a 2x2 square in the XY plane.

Let me fix this: The edge geometry should be defined in local coordinates (Z=0 for all vertices since it's a flat face in XY plane), and then the wireframe mesh will be positioned/rotated along with the face.

Actually, looking more carefully: The face is a PlaneGeometry(2,2) which creates a 2x2 plane in the XY plane (Z=0). The face is then rotated and positioned. The wireframe should match this - so the edges should be the four edges of a 2x2 square in the XY plane at Z=0.

So the local edge coordinates should be:
- Bottom: (-1, -1, 0) to (1, -1, 0)
- Right: (1, -1, 0) to (1, 1, 0)
- Top: (1, 1, 0) to (-1, 1, 0)
- Left: (-1, 1, 0) to (-1, -1, 0)

This is the same for ALL faces since they all start as the same PlaneGeometry in local space, then get rotated/positioned differently.

Let me rewrite the faceData to use local edge coordinates, and fix the wireframe creation.

import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

// Scene setup

scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xffffff); // White background

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(0, 0, 5);

renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Controls

controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.enablePan = false;
controls.minDistance = 3;
controls.maxDistance = 10;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;

// Create cube as 6 separate face meshes
const faceSize = 2;
const halfSize = faceSize / 2;
const faces = [];
const faceWireframes = []; // Wireframe per face

// Face data: [position, rotation, normal]
// All faces use the same local edge geometry (2x2 square in XY plane at Z=0)
const faceData = [
    // Front (+Z)
    { pos: [0, 0, halfSize], rot: [0, 0, 0], normal: [0, 0, 1] },
    // Back (-Z)
    { pos: [0, 0, -halfSize], rot: [0, Math.PI, 0], normal: [0, 0, -1] },
    // Right (+X)
    { pos: [halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, -Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [1, 0, 0] },
    // Left (-X)
    { pos: [-halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [-1, 0, 0] },
    // Top (+Y)
    { pos: [0, halfSize, 0], rot: [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, 1, 0] },
    // Bottom (-Y)
    { pos: [0, -halfSize, 0], rot: [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, -1, 0] },
];

const faceGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(faceSize, faceSize);

// Local edge geometry for a 2x2 face (in XY plane at Z=0)
// This matches the PlaneGeometry exactly
const localEdges = new Float32Array([
    // Bottom edge
    -halfSize, -halfSize, 0,
    halfSize, -halfSize, 0,
    // Right edge
    halfSize, -halfSize, 0,
    halfSize, halfSize, 0,
    // Top edge
    halfSize, halfSize, 0,
    -halfSize, halfSize, 0,
    // Left edge
    -halfSize, halfSize, 0,
    -halfSize, -halfSize, 0,
]);

const edgeGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
edgeGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(localEdges, 3));

// Crack texture generator - produces visible (map) and alpha (alphaMap) textures
class CrackTexture {
    constructor(size = 512) {
        this.size = size;
        
        // Visible texture: black glass background with bright refraction cracks
        this.visibleCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.visibleCanvas.width = size;
        this.visibleCanvas.height = size;
        this.visibleCtx = this.visibleCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.visibleTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.visibleCanvas);
        this.visibleTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.visibleTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.visibleTexture.anisotropy = 4;
        
        // Alpha texture: white background with black cracks (for shatter pieces)
        this.alphaCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.alphaCanvas.width = size;
        this.alphaCanvas.height = size;
        this.alphaCtx = this.alphaCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.alphaTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.alphaCanvas);
        this.alphaTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.alphaTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        
        this.cracks = []; // Array of crack paths
        this.impactPoints = []; // Impact points for radial cracks
        this.clear();
    }

    clear() {
        // Visible: deep black glass base
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        
        // Alpha: white background (opaque)
        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        
        this.cracks = [];
        this.impactPoints = [];
        this.updateTextures();
    }

    // Add impact point for radial crack pattern
    addImpact(x, y) {
        const tx = (x / this.size) * this.size;
        const ty = (y / this.size) * this.size;
        this.impactPoints.push({ x: tx, y: ty });
    }

    // Generate a lightning-like crack path from impact points
    generateCrack() {
        const path = [];
        
        let startX, startY;
        if (this.impactPoints.length > 0 && Math.random() < 0.7) {
            const impact = this.impactPoints[Math.floor(Math.random() * this.impactPoints.length)];
            startX = impact.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
            startY = impact.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
        } else {
            startX = Math.random() * this.size;
            startY = Math.random() * this.size;
        }
        path.push({ x: startX, y: startY });

        const segments = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
        let x = startX;
        let y = startY;
        let angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
            const length = 15 + Math.random() * 35;
            angle += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5;
            x += Math.cos(angle) * length;
            y += Math.sin(angle) * length;

            if (x < 0) { x = Math.abs(x); angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (x > this.size) { x = 2 * this.size - x; angle = Math.PI - angle; }
            if (y < 0) { y = Math.abs(y); angle = -angle; }
            if (y > this.size) { y = 2 * this.size - y; angle = -angle; }

            path.push({ x, y });

            if (Math.random() < 0.25 && i > 1) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() - 0.5) * Math.PI * 0.8;
                const branchLength = 10 + Math.random() * 25;
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
            
            if (Math.random() < 0.15 && i > 2) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (Math.PI/2 + Math.random() * Math.PI/4);
                const branchLength = 5 + Math.random() * 15;
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
        }
        this.cracks.push(path);
    }

    addCracks(count, impactX, impactY) {
        if (impactX !== undefined && impactY !== undefined) {
            this.addImpact(impactX, impactY);
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            this.generateCrack();
        }
        this.redraw();
    }

    redraw() {
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        this.impactPoints.forEach(pt => {
            const grad = this.visibleCtx.createRadialGradient(pt.x, pt.y, 0, pt.x, pt.y, 8);
            grad.addColorStop(0, '#1a1a1a');
            grad.addColorStop(1, '#000000');
            this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = grad;
            this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
            this.visibleCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.visibleCtx.fill();
            
            this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
            this.alphaCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.alphaCtx.fill();
        });

        this.cracks.forEach((path, pathIndex) => {
            if (path.length < 2) return;
            
            const isMain = pathIndex < this.cracks.length * 0.3;
            const baseWidth = isMain ? 2.5 : 1.2;
            
            // VISIBLE TEXTURE: Glass refraction cracks
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#0a0a0a';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth + 1;
            this.visibleCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.visibleCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.4;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#444444';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 1.8;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
            this.visibleCtx.filter = 'blur(1px)';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.filter = 'none';
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#cccccc';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.15;
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);

            // ALPHA TEXTURE
            this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth + 1.5;
            this.alphaCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.alphaCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.drawPath(this.alphaCtx, path);
        });

        this.cracks.forEach(path => {
            if (path.length < 3) return;
            for (let i = 1; i < path.length - 1; i += 3) {
                if (Math.random() < 0.4) {
                    const px = path[i].x;
                    const py = path[i].y;
                    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                    const len = 3 + Math.random() * 12;
                    
                    this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#888888';
                    this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = 0.6;
                    this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
                    this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
                    this.visibleCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.visibleCtx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.visibleCtx.stroke();
                    this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
                    
                    this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
                    this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = 1.0;
                    this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
                    this.alphaCtx.moveTo(px, py);
                    this.alphaCtx.lineTo(px + Math.cos(angle) * len, py + Math.sin(angle) * len);
                    this.alphaCtx.stroke();
                }
            }
        });

        this.updateTextures();
    }

    drawPath(ctx, path) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(path[0].x, path[0].y);
        for (let i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
            const xc = (path[i-1].x + path[i].x) / 2;
            const yc = (path[i-1].y + path[i].y) / 2;
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(path[i-1].x, path[i-1].y, xc, yc);
        }
        if (path.length > 1) {
            const last = path[path.length - 1];
            const prev = path[path.length - 2];
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(prev.x, prev.y, last.x, last.y);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
    }

    updateTextures() {
        this.visibleTexture.needsUpdate = true;
        this.alphaTexture.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    getVisibleTexture() {
        return this.visibleTexture;
    }

    getAlphaTexture() {
        return this.alphaTexture;
    }
}

    face.userData = { 
        faceIndex: index, 
        originalPosition: data.pos, 
        originalRotation: data.rot,
        clickCount: 0,
        maxClicks: 4,
        crackTexture: crackTexture,
    };
    scene.add(face);
    faces.push(face);

    // Create wireframe for this face using shared local edge geometry
    // The wireframe will be positioned/rotated exactly like the face
    const edgeMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ 
        color: 0x333333, 
        transparent: true, 
        opacity: 0.4 
    });
    
    const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgeGeometry, edgeMaterial);
    wireframe.position.set(...data.pos);
    wireframe.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    scene.add(wireframe);
    faceWireframes.push(wireframe);
});

// Group for rotation (contains faces + wireframes)
const cubeGroup = new THREE.Group();
faces.forEach(face => cubeGroup.add(face));
faceWireframes.forEach(wf => cubeGroup.add(wf));
scene.add(cubeGroup);

// Lighting
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.6);
scene.add(ambientLight);

const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.0);
directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
scene.add(directionalLight);

const fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.6);
fillLight.position.set(-5, 5, -7);
scene.add(fillLight);

const topLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
topLight.position.set(0, 10, 0);
scene.add(topLight);

const sideLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
sideLight1.position.set(10, 0, 5);
scene.add(sideLight1);

const sideLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
sideLight2.position.set(-10, 0, -5);
scene.add(sideLight2);

const sparkleLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffffff, 0.3, 10);
sparkleLight.position.set(0, 5, 5);
scene.add(sparkleLight);

// Raycaster
const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

function onClick(event) {
    mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
    mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

    raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
    const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(faces.filter(f => f.visible), true);

    if (intersects.length > 0) {
        const hit = intersects[0];
        const hitFace = hit.object;
        const uv = hit.uv;
        
        const texX = uv.x * 512;
        const texY = (1 - uv.y) * 512;
        
        handleFaceClick(hitFace, texX, texY);
    }
}

function handleFaceClick(face, texX, texY) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    userData.clickCount++;
    
    const cracksToAdd = [3, 6, 10, 0][userData.clickCount - 1] || 0;
    if (cracksToAdd > 0) {
        userData.crackTexture.addCracks(cracksToAdd, texX, texY);
    }
    
    createImpactFlash(face, texX, texY);
    
    if (userData.clickCount >= userData.maxClicks) {
        animateFaceShatter(face);
    }
}

function createImpactFlash(face, texX, texY) {
    const flashGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(0.01, 0.15, 32);
    const flashMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: 0xffffff,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.8,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        depthWrite: false,
    });
    const flash = new THREE.Mesh(flashGeo, flashMat);
    
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldNormal = new THREE.Vector3();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldDirection(worldNormal);
    flash.position.copy(worldPos).addScaledVector(worldNormal, 0.011);
    flash.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), worldNormal);
    
    scene.add(flash);
    
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 150;
    
    function animateFlash() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
        
        flash.material.opacity = 0.8 * (1 - eased);
        flash.scale.setScalar(1 + eased * 3);
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animateFlash);
        } else {
            scene.remove(flash);
            flash.geometry.dispose();
            flash.material.dispose();
        }
    }
    animateFlash();
}

function animateFaceShatter(face) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    const crackTexture = userData.crackTexture;
    const faceIndex = userData.faceIndex;
    
    // Hide the corresponding wireframe immediately
    if (faceWireframes[faceIndex]) {
        faceWireframes[faceIndex].visible = false;
    }
    
    const pieces = createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture);
    
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 800;
    
    function animatePieces() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = Math.pow(progress, 2.5);
        
        pieces.forEach((piece, i) => {
            const delay = i * 0.015;
            const pieceProgress = Math.max(0, (progress - delay) / (1 - delay));
            if (pieceProgress <= 0) return;
            
            const pieceEased = Math.pow(pieceProgress, 2);
            
            piece.position.x += piece.userData.velocity.x * pieceEased * 0.8;
            piece.position.y += piece.userData.velocity.y * pieceEased * 0.8;
            piece.position.z += piece.userData.velocity.z * pieceEased * 0.8;
            
            piece.rotation.x += piece.userData.angularVelocity.x * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.y += piece.userData.angularVelocity.y * pieceEased;
            piece.rotation.z += piece.userData.angularVelocity.z * pieceEased;
            
            piece.material.opacity = 1 - pieceEased;
            piece.material.transparent = true;
            piece.material.needsUpdate = true;
        });
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animatePieces);
        } else {
            pieces.forEach(piece => {
                scene.remove(piece);
                piece.geometry.dispose();
                piece.material.dispose();
            });
            face.visible = false;
            crackTexture.clear();
            userData.clickCount = 0;
        }
    }
    
    face.visible = false;
    animatePieces();
}

function createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture) {
    const pieces = [];
    const pieceCount = 15 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
    
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldQuaternion(worldQuat);
    
    for (let i = 0; i < pieceCount; i++) {
        const size = 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.18;
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size, size, 0.03);
        
        const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x000000,
            roughness: 0.1,
            metalness: 0.0,
            side: THREE.DoubleSide,
            map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(),
            alphaMap: crackTexture.getAlphaTexture(),
            transparent: true,
            alphaTest: 0.01,
            clearcoat: 1.0,
            clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
            transmission: 0.9,
            thickness: 0.3,
            ior: 1.5,
        });
        mat.needsUpdate = true;
        
        const piece = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        
        const offset = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1
        );
        offset.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        piece.position.copy(worldPos).add(offset);
        piece.quaternion.copy(worldQuat);
        
        const dir = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
            -0.2 - Math.random() * 0.3
        ).normalize();
        const speed = 0.015 + Math.random() * 0.025;
        const velocity = dir.multiplyScalar(speed);
        velocity.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        
        const angularVelocity = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08
        );
        
        piece.userData = { velocity, angularVelocity };
        scene.add(piece);
        pieces.push(piece);
    }
    
    return pieces;
}

window.addEventListener('click', onClick);

// Minimal Audio Engine
class AudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.15;
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    }

    start() {
        if (this.isPlaying) return;
        this.isPlaying = true;
        this.scheduleLoop();
    }

    stop() {
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    scheduleLoop() {
        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81];
        frequencies.forEach((freq) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

// Resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// Animation loop
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    
    controls.update();
    
    cubeGroup.rotation.x += 0.0005;
    cubeGroup.rotation.y += 0.0008;
    
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    faceWireframes.forEach(wf => {
        wf.material.opacity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.1;
    });
    
    sparkleLight.position.x = Math.sin(time * 0.7) * 3;
    sparkleLight.position.z = Math.cos(time * 0.5) * 3 + 5;
    
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

animate();
