import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

// Scene setup
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x000000);

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
camera.position.set(0, 0, 5);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Controls
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.enablePan = false;
controls.minDistance = 3;
controls.maxDistance = 10;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;

// State management
const state = {
    phase: 'cube', // 'cube', 'well', 'falling'
    facesDestroyed: 0,
    wellClicked: false,
    fallProgress: 0,
    fallStartTime: 0,
    fallSpeed: 0,
};

// Create cube as 6 separate face meshes
const faceSize = 2;
const halfSize = faceSize / 2;
const faces = [];
const faceWireframes = [];

const faceData = [
    { pos: [0, 0, halfSize], rot: [0, 0, 0], normal: [0, 0, 1] },
    { pos: [0, 0, -halfSize], rot: [0, Math.PI, 0], normal: [0, 0, -1] },
    { pos: [halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, -Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [1, 0, 0] },
    { pos: [-halfSize, 0, 0], rot: [0, Math.PI / 2, 0], normal: [-1, 0, 0] },
    { pos: [0, halfSize, 0], rot: [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, 1, 0] },
    { pos: [0, -halfSize, 0], rot: [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, -1, 0] },
];

const faceGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(faceSize, faceSize);

const localEdges = new Float32Array([
    -halfSize, -halfSize, 0,  halfSize, -halfSize, 0,
    halfSize, -halfSize, 0,  halfSize, halfSize, 0,
    halfSize, halfSize, 0,  -halfSize, halfSize, 0,
    -halfSize, halfSize, 0,  -halfSize, -halfSize, 0,
]);

const edgeGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
edgeGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(localEdges, 3));

// Crack texture generator
class CrackTexture {
    constructor(size = 512) {
        this.size = size;
        this.visibleCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.visibleCanvas.width = size;
        this.visibleCanvas.height = size;
        this.visibleCtx = this.visibleCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.visibleTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.visibleCanvas);
        this.visibleTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.visibleTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        
        this.alphaCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.alphaCanvas.width = size;
        this.alphaCanvas.height = size;
        this.alphaCtx = this.alphaCanvas.getContext('2d');
        this.alphaTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.alphaCanvas);
        this.alphaTexture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        this.alphaTexture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        
        this.cracks = [];
        this.impactPoints = [];
        this.clear();
    }

    clear() {
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        this.cracks = [];
        this.impactPoints = [];
        this.updateTextures();
    }

    addImpact(x, y) {
        this.impactPoints.push({ x, y });
    }

    generateWindshieldCracks(impactX, impactY, severity) {
        const newCracks = [];
        const radialCount = 3 + severity * 2;
        for (let i = 0; i < radialCount; i++) {
            const baseAngle = (i / radialCount) * Math.PI * 2;
            const angleVariation = (Math.random() - 0.5) * (Math.PI / radialCount) * 0.5;
            const angle = baseAngle + angleVariation;
            const path = this.generateRadialCrack(impactX, impactY, angle, severity);
            if (path.length > 1) newCracks.push(path);
            if (Math.random() < 0.3 && severity > 0) {
                const branchAngle = angle + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8;
                const branchPath = this.generateRadialCrack(impactX, impactY, branchAngle, severity - 0.5, 0.5);
                if (branchPath.length > 1) newCracks.push(branchPath);
            }
        }
        const ringCount = severity;
        for (let r = 0; r < ringCount; r++) {
            const radius = 20 + r * 30 + severity * 15 + Math.random() * 10;
            const ringCracks = this.generateConcentricRing(impactX, impactY, radius, severity);
            newCracks.push(...ringCracks);
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < 2 + severity; i++) {
            const chipAngle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            const chipDist = Math.random() * 8;
            const chipX = impactX + Math.cos(chipAngle) * chipDist;
            const chipY = impactY + Math.sin(chipAngle) * chipDist;
            const chipPath = this.generateChipCrack(chipX, chipY);
            if (chipPath.length > 1) newCracks.push(chipPath);
        }
        this.cracks.push(...newCracks);
    }

    generateRadialCrack(cx, cy, angle, severity, lengthMult = 1.0) {
        const path = [{ x: cx, y: cy }];
        const maxLength = (80 + severity * 60) * lengthMult;
        let x = cx, y = cy;
        let currentAngle = angle;
        let traveled = 0;
        while (traveled < maxLength) {
            const step = 8 + Math.random() * 15;
            const angleJitter = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5 * (1 + severity * 0.3);
            currentAngle += angleJitter;
            x += Math.cos(currentAngle) * step;
            y += Math.sin(currentAngle) * step;
            traveled += step;
            if (x < 0) { x = Math.abs(x); currentAngle = Math.PI - currentAngle; }
            if (x > this.size) { x = 2 * this.size - x; currentAngle = Math.PI - currentAngle; }
            if (y < 0) { y = Math.abs(y); currentAngle = -currentAngle; }
            if (y > this.size) { y = 2 * this.size - y; currentAngle = -currentAngle; }
            path.push({ x, y });
            if (Math.random() < 0.1 * (1 + severity) && traveled > 20) {
                const branchAngle = currentAngle + (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (Math.PI/2.5 + Math.random() * Math.PI/2.5);
                const branchLength = 15 + Math.random() * 25 * (0.5 + severity * 0.3);
                const bx = x + Math.cos(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                const by = y + Math.sin(branchAngle) * branchLength;
                this.cracks.push([{ x, y }, { x: bx, y: by }]);
            }
            if (x < 10 || x > this.size - 10 || y < 10 || y > this.size - 10) break;
        }
        return path;
    }

    generateConcentricRing(cx, cy, radius, severity) {
        const cracks = [];
        const segments = 6 + Math.floor(severity * 2) + Math.floor(radius / 15);
        for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
            const startAngle = (i / segments) * Math.PI * 2;
            const endAngle = ((i + 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.3) / segments) * Math.PI * 2;
            if (Math.random() < 0.6 + severity * 0.1) {
                const path = [];
                const steps = 4 + Math.floor((endAngle - startAngle) * 8);
                for (let s = 0; s <= steps; s++) {
                    const a = startAngle + (endAngle - startAngle) * (s / steps);
                    const r = radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6 * (1 + severity * 0.4);
                    const x = cx + Math.cos(a) * r;
                    const y = cy + Math.sin(a) * r;
                    if (x > 5 && x < this.size - 5 && y > 5 && y < this.size - 5) {
                        path.push({ x, y });
                    }
                }
                if (path.length > 2) cracks.push(path);
            }
        }
        return cracks;
    }

    generateChipCrack(cx, cy) {
        const path = [{ x: cx, y: cy }];
        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const length = 5 + Math.random() * 15;
        const x = cx + Math.cos(angle) * length;
        const y = cy + Math.sin(angle) * length;
        path.push({ x, y });
        return path;
    }

    addCracks(severity, impactX, impactY) {
        const severityLevel = Math.min(severity, 4) - 1;
        if (impactX !== undefined && impactY !== undefined) {
            this.addImpact(impactX, impactY);
            this.generateWindshieldCracks(impactX, impactY, severityLevel);
        }
        this.redraw();
    }

    redraw() {
        this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
        this.visibleCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
        this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'white';
        this.alphaCtx.fillRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);

        this.cracks.forEach((path, pathIndex) => {
            if (path.length < 2) return;
            const isMain = pathIndex < this.cracks.length * 0.4;
            const baseWidth = isMain ? 2.5 : 1.5;
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#000000';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth + 1;
            this.visibleCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.visibleCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.visibleCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';
            this.visibleCtx.shadowBlur = 2;
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.shadowBlur = 0;
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.4;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#444444';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.8;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'overlay';
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#aaaaaa';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth * 0.15;
            this.drawPath(this.visibleCtx, path);

            this.alphaCtx.strokeStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.lineWidth = baseWidth + 2;
            this.alphaCtx.lineCap = 'round';
            this.alphaCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
            this.drawPath(this.alphaCtx, path);
        });

        this.impactPoints.forEach(pt => {
            const grad = this.visibleCtx.createRadialGradient(pt.x, pt.y, 0, pt.x, pt.y, 6);
            grad.addColorStop(0, '#000000');
            grad.addColorStop(0.5, '#222222');
            grad.addColorStop(1, '#000000');
            this.visibleCtx.fillStyle = grad;
            this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
            this.visibleCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.visibleCtx.fill();
            
            this.visibleCtx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            this.visibleCtx.lineWidth = 1.5;
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
            this.visibleCtx.beginPath();
            this.visibleCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.visibleCtx.stroke();
            this.visibleCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            
            this.alphaCtx.fillStyle = 'black';
            this.alphaCtx.beginPath();
            this.alphaCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            this.alphaCtx.fill();
        });

        this.updateTextures();
    }

    drawPath(ctx, path) {
        if (path.length < 2) return;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(path[0].x, path[0].y);
        for (let i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
            const xc = (path[i-1].x + path[i].x) / 2;
            const yc = (path[i-1].y + path[i].y) / 2;
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(path[i-1].x, path[i-1].y, xc, yc);
        }
        if (path.length > 1) {
            const last = path[path.length - 1];
            const prev = path[path.length - 2];
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(prev.x, prev.y, last.x, last.y);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
    }

    updateTextures() {
        this.visibleTexture.needsUpdate = true;
        this.alphaTexture.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    getVisibleTexture() { return this.visibleTexture; }
    getAlphaTexture() { return this.alphaTexture; }
}

// Well shader - dark stone with glowing bottom
const wellVertexShader = `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    varying vec3 vWorldPosition;
    varying vec3 vNormal;
    void main() {
        vUv = uv;
        vWorldPosition = (modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0)).xyz;
        vNormal = normalize(normalMatrix * normal);
        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
    }
`;

const wellFragmentShader = `
    uniform float uTime;
    uniform float uGlowIntensity;
    varying vec2 vUv;
    varying vec3 vWorldPosition;
    varying vec3 vNormal;
    
    float hash(vec2 p) {
        return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);
    }
    
    float noise(vec2 p) {
        vec2 i = floor(p);
        vec2 f = fract(p);
        f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
        return mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), f.x),
                   mix(hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), f.x), f.y);
    }
    
    float fbm(vec2 p) {
        float value = 0.0;
        float amplitude = 0.5;
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            value += amplitude * noise(p);
            p *= 2.0;
            amplitude *= 0.5;
        }
        return value;
    }
    
    void main() {
        // Stone texture
        float stone = fbm(vUv * 10.0 + vec2(uTime * 0.02, 0.0));
        stone += fbm(vUv * 20.0) * 0.5;
        stone += fbm(vUv * 40.0) * 0.25;
        
        // Vertical stratification
        float strata = sin(vUv.y * 30.0 + stone * 5.0) * 0.1;
        
        // Base stone color - dark, cold
        vec3 stoneColor = vec3(0.08, 0.07, 0.09) + stone * 0.03 + strata * 0.02;
        
        // Moss/dampness in cracks
        float cracks = fbm(vUv * 50.0);
        cracks = step(0.7, cracks);
        stoneColor += cracks * vec3(0.02, 0.04, 0.01);
        
        // Glowing bottom - gets stronger near bottom
        float depthFactor = 1.0 - smoothstep(-1.5, 0.5, vWorldPosition.y);
        float glow = depthFactor * uGlowIntensity;
        
        // Pulsing glow
        glow *= 0.7 + 0.3 * sin(uTime * 2.0 + vWorldPosition.y * 5.0);
        
        // Color shifts to warm/orange near bottom
        vec3 glowColor = mix(vec3(0.3, 0.1, 0.0), vec3(1.0, 0.4, 0.1), pow(depthFactor, 2.0));
        stoneColor += glow * glowColor;
        
        // Subtle particles rising
        float particles = 0.0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            float p = noise(vec2(vUv.x * 100.0 + uTime * (0.5 + float(i) * 0.2), vUv.y * 50.0 - uTime * (0.3 + float(i) * 0.1) + float(i) * 100.0));
            particles += pow(p, 10.0) * 0.1;
        }
        stoneColor += particles * vec3(0.5, 0.3, 0.1) * depthFactor;
        
        // Rim lighting
        float rim = 1.0 - max(0.0, dot(vNormal, vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)));
        stoneColor += rim * 0.05 * vec3(0.2, 0.1, 0.05);
        
        gl_FragColor = vec4(stoneColor, 1.0);
    }
`;

// Falling tunnel shader
const tunnelVertexShader = `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    varying vec3 vWorldPosition;
    void main() {
        vUv = uv;
        vWorldPosition = (modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0)).xyz;
        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
    }
`;

const tunnelFragmentShader = `
    uniform float uTime;
    uniform float uFallProgress;
    uniform float uFallSpeed;
    varying vec2 vUv;
    varying vec3 vWorldPosition;
    
    float hash(vec2 p) {
        return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);
    }
    
    float noise(vec2 p) {
        vec2 i = floor(p);
        vec2 f = fract(p);
        f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
        return mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), f.x),
                   mix(hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), f.x), f.y);
    }
    
    float fbm(vec2 p) {
        float value = 0.0;
        float amplitude = 0.5;
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            value += amplitude * noise(p);
            p *= 2.0;
            amplitude *= 0.5;
        }
        return value;
    }
    
    void main() {
        vec2 uv = vUv;
        float depth = vWorldPosition.z;
        
        // Falling motion
        float fallOffset = uFallSpeed * uTime * 50.0;
        float tunnelDepth = mod(depth + fallOffset, 40.0) - 20.0;
        
        // Tunnel radius breathes
        float radius = 0.6 + 0.3 * sin(tunnelDepth * 0.3 + uTime * 0.5);
        radius += fbm(vec2(tunnelDepth * 0.2, uTime * 0.1)) * 0.15;
        
        // Distance from center
        vec2 center = vec2(0.5, 0.5);
        float dist = length(uv - center) / radius;
        
        // Tunnel walls
        float wall = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.85, 1.0, dist);
        
        // Spiraling
        float angle = atan(uv.y - 0.5, uv.x - 0.5);
        float spiral = angle * 3.0 + tunnelDepth * 0.4 + uTime * 0.5;
        
        // Layered patterns
        float n1 = fbm(vec2(spiral * 0.3, tunnelDepth * 0.15 + uTime * 0.05));
        float n2 = fbm(vec2(spiral * 0.6, tunnelDepth * 0.3 - uTime * 0.08));
        float n3 = fbm(vec2(spiral * 1.2, tunnelDepth * 0.6 + uTime * 0.03));
        
        float pattern = n1 * 0.5 + n2 * 0.3 + n3 * 0.2;
        pattern = pow(pattern, 1.5);
        
        // Color palette shifts with depth and progress
        float depthCycle = fract(tunnelDepth * 0.05 + uFallProgress * 0.5);
        vec3 color1 = mix(vec3(0.02, 0.01, 0.03), vec3(0.05, 0.02, 0.08), depthCycle);
        vec3 color2 = mix(vec3(0.08, 0.03, 0.12), vec3(0.15, 0.05, 0.2), depthCycle);
        vec3 color3 = mix(vec3(0.2, 0.08, 0.15), vec3(0.4, 0.15, 0.25), depthCycle);
        
        vec3 color = mix(color1, color2, fract(tunnelDepth * 0.1));
        color = mix(color, color3, pattern * 0.6);
        color += pattern * 0.3 * color1;
        
        // Depth fog - darker deeper
        float fog = exp(-abs(tunnelDepth) * 0.08) * (1.0 - uFallProgress * 0.3);
        color *= fog;
        
        // Glowing rings at intervals
        float rings = sin(tunnelDepth * 1.5 + uTime * 2.0) * 0.5 + 0.5;
        rings = pow(rings, 15.0) * 0.4 * (1.0 - uFallProgress * 0.5);
        color += rings * color2;
        
        // Particles rushing past
        float particles = 0.0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            float p = noise(vec2(spiral * 0.2 + float(i) * 10.0, tunnelDepth * 0.4 + uTime * (1.5 + float(i) * 0.3) + float(i) * 50.0));
            particles += pow(p, 20.0) * 0.15;
        }
        color += particles * vec3(1.0, 0.6, 0.3);
        
        // Vignette intensifies with fall
        float vignette = 1.0 - length(uv - 0.5) * (1.5 + uFallProgress * 2.0);
        color *= vignette;
        
        // Center glow - the "light at the end"
        float centerGlow = exp(-length(uv - 0.5) * 20.0) * uFallProgress * 0.5;
        color += centerGlow * vec3(1.0, 0.8, 0.4);
        
        float alpha = wall * (0.8 + 0.2 * pattern) * fog;
        
        gl_FragColor = vec4(color, alpha);
    }
`;

// Create cube faces
faceData.forEach((data, index) => {
    const crackTexture = new CrackTexture(512);
    
    const faceMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: 0x000000,
        roughness: 0.35,
        metalness: 0.0,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(),
    });
    
    const face = new THREE.Mesh(faceGeometry, faceMaterial);
    face.position.set(...data.pos);
    face.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    face.userData = { 
        faceIndex: index, 
        originalPosition: data.pos, 
        originalRotation: data.rot,
        clickCount: 0,
        maxClicks: 4,
        crackTexture: crackTexture,
    };
    scene.add(face);
    faces.push(face);

    const edgeMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ 
        color: 0x333333, 
        transparent: true, 
        opacity: 0.4 
    });
    
    const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgeGeometry, edgeMaterial);
    wireframe.position.set(...data.pos);
    wireframe.rotation.set(...data.rot);
    scene.add(wireframe);
    faceWireframes.push(wireframe);
});

// Well mesh - cylinder with open top and bottom
const wellGeometry = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.5, 1.5, 4, 32, 10, true);
wellGeometry.scale(1, 1, -1); // Invert normals to see inside
const wellMaterial = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
    vertexShader: wellVertexShader,
    fragmentShader: wellFragmentShader,
    uniforms: {
        uTime: { value: 0 },
        uGlowIntensity: { value: 0 },
    },
    transparent: true,
    depthWrite: false,
    side: THREE.BackSide,
});
const well = new THREE.Mesh(wellGeometry, wellMaterial);
well.position.set(0, -2, 0); // Centered where cube was
well.visible = false;
scene.add(well);

// Well rim - top edge
const rimGeometry = new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.5, 0.1, 8, 32);
const rimMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color: 0x1a1510,
    roughness: 0.9,
    metalness: 0.1,
});
const wellRim = new THREE.Mesh(rimGeometry, rimMaterial);
wellRim.position.set(0, 0, 0);
wellRim.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
wellRim.visible = false;
scene.add(wellRim);

// Well particles - rising motes
const wellParticles = new THREE.Group();
const wellParticleCount = 150;
const wellParticleGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
const wellParticlePositions = new Float32Array(wellParticleCount * 3);
const wellParticleVelocities = new Float32Array(wellParticleCount * 3);
const wellParticleSizes = new Float32Array(wellParticleCount);
const wellParticlePhases = new Float32Array(wellParticleCount);

for (let i = 0; i < wellParticleCount; i++) {
    const radius = 0.2 + Math.random() * 1.2;
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const y = -2 + Math.random() * 4;
    
    wellParticlePositions[i * 3] = Math.cos(angle) * radius;
    wellParticlePositions[i * 3 + 1] = y;
    wellParticlePositions[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(angle) * radius;
    
    wellParticleVelocities[i * 3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.002;
    wellParticleVelocities[i * 3 + 1] = 0.005 + Math.random() * 0.01; // Rising
    wellParticleVelocities[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.002;
    
    wellParticleSizes[i] = 0.01 + Math.random() * 0.02;
    wellParticlePhases[i] = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
}

wellParticleGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(wellParticlePositions, 3));
wellParticleGeometry.setAttribute('velocity', new THREE.BufferAttribute(wellParticleVelocities, 3));
wellParticleGeometry.setAttribute('size', new THREE.BufferAttribute(wellParticleSizes, 1));
wellParticleGeometry.setAttribute('phase', new THREE.BufferAttribute(wellParticlePhases, 1));

const wellParticleMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
    color: 0xff8844,
    size: 0.03,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0,
    depthWrite: false,
    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
    sizeAttenuation: true,
});

const wellParticleSystem = new THREE.Points(wellParticleGeometry, wellParticleMaterial);
wellParticleSystem.visible = false;
scene.add(wellParticleSystem);
wellParticles.add(wellParticleSystem);

// Well light - emanating from below
const wellLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xff6622, 0, 10);
wellLight.position.set(0, -4, 0);
scene.add(wellLight);

// Falling tunnel
const tunnelGeometry = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1, 1, 200, 32, 100, true);
tunnelGeometry.scale(1, 1, -1);
const tunnelMaterial = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
    vertexShader: tunnelVertexShader,
    fragmentShader: tunnelFragmentShader,
    uniforms: {
        uTime: { value: 0 },
        uFallProgress: { value: 0 },
        uFallSpeed: { value: 1 },
    },
    transparent: true,
    depthWrite: false,
    side: THREE.BackSide,
});
const tunnel = new THREE.Mesh(tunnelGeometry, tunnelMaterial);
tunnel.position.z = -100;
tunnel.visible = false;
scene.add(tunnel);

// Tunnel particles
const tunnelParticles = new THREE.Group();
const tunnelParticleCount = 800;
const tunnelParticleGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
const tunnelParticlePositions = new Float32Array(tunnelParticleCount * 3);
const tunnelParticleVelocities = new Float32Array(tunnelParticleCount * 3);
const tunnelParticleSizes = new Float32Array(tunnelParticleCount);
const tunnelParticleColors = new Float32Array(tunnelParticleCount * 3);

for (let i = 0; i < tunnelParticleCount; i++) {
    const radius = 0.1 + Math.random() * 0.9;
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const z = -Math.random() * 200;
    
    tunnelParticlePositions[i * 3] = Math.cos(angle) * radius;
    tunnelParticlePositions[i * 3 + 1] = Math.sin(angle) * radius;
    tunnelParticlePositions[i * 3 + 2] = z;
    
    tunnelParticleVelocities[i * 3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.01;
    tunnelParticleVelocities[i * 3 + 1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.01;
    tunnelParticleVelocities[i * 3 + 2] = 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.5;
    
    tunnelParticleSizes[i] = 0.005 + Math.random() * 0.02;
    
    const colorChoice = Math.random();
    if (colorChoice < 0.4) {
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3] = 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.5;
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3 + 1] = 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.3;
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3 + 2] = 0.1;
    } else if (colorChoice < 0.7) {
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3] = 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4;
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3 + 1] = 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4;
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3 + 2] = 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.3;
    } else {
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3] = 0.1 + Math.random() * 0.2;
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3 + 1] = 0.1 + Math.random() * 0.2;
        tunnelParticleColors[i * 3 + 2] = 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.3;
    }
}

tunnelParticleGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(tunnelParticlePositions, 3));
tunnelParticleGeometry.setAttribute('velocity', new THREE.BufferAttribute(tunnelParticleVelocities, 3));
tunnelParticleGeometry.setAttribute('size', new THREE.BufferAttribute(tunnelParticleSizes, 1));
tunnelParticleGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(tunnelParticleColors, 3));

const tunnelParticleMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
    size: 0.02,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0,
    depthWrite: false,
    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
    sizeAttenuation: true,
    vertexColors: true,
});

const tunnelParticleSystem = new THREE.Points(tunnelParticleGeometry, tunnelParticleMaterial);
tunnelParticleSystem.visible = false;
scene.add(tunnelParticleSystem);
tunnelParticles.add(tunnelParticleSystem);

// Group for rotation
const cubeGroup = new THREE.Group();
faces.forEach(face => cubeGroup.add(face));
faceWireframes.forEach(wf => cubeGroup.add(wf));
scene.add(cubeGroup);

// Lighting
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.3);
scene.add(ambientLight);

const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
scene.add(directionalLight);

const fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.3);
fillLight.position.set(-5, 5, -7);
scene.add(fillLight);

const topLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.2);
topLight.position.set(0, 10, 0);
scene.add(topLight);

const sideLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.2);
sideLight1.position.set(10, 0, 5);
scene.add(sideLight1);

const sideLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.2);
sideLight2.position.set(-10, 0, -5);
scene.add(sideLight2);

// Raycaster
const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

function onClick(event) {
    mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
    mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

    if (state.phase === 'cube') {
        raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(faces.filter(f => f.visible), true);
        if (intersects.length > 0) {
            const hit = intersects[0];
            const hitFace = hit.object;
            const uv = hit.uv;
            const texX = uv.x * 512;
            const texY = (1 - uv.y) * 512;
            handleFaceClick(hitFace, texX, texY);
        }
    } else if (state.phase === 'well' && !state.wellClicked) {
        raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects([well, wellRim]);
        if (intersects.length > 0) {
            activateWell();
        }
    }
}

function handleFaceClick(face, texX, texY) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    userData.clickCount++;
    
    const severity = userData.clickCount;
    userData.crackTexture.addCracks(severity, texX, texY);
    
    createImpactFlash(face, texX, texY);
    
    if (userData.clickCount >= userData.maxClicks) {
        animateFaceShatter(face);
    }
}

function createImpactFlash(face, texX, texY) {
    const flashGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(0.01, 0.15, 32);
    const flashMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: 0xffffff,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.8,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        depthWrite: false,
    });
    const flash = new THREE.Mesh(flashGeo, flashMat);
    
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldNormal = new THREE.Vector3();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldDirection(worldNormal);
    flash.position.copy(worldPos).addScaledVector(worldNormal, 0.011);
    flash.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), worldNormal);
    
    scene.add(flash);
    
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 150;
    
    function animateFlash() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
        
        flash.material.opacity = 0.8 * (1 - eased);
        flash.scale.setScalar(1 + eased * 3);
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animateFlash);
        } else {
            scene.remove(flash);
            flash.geometry.dispose();
            flash.material.dispose();
        }
    }
    animateFlash();
}

function animateFaceShatter(face) {
    const userData = face.userData;
    const crackTexture = userData.crackTexture;
    const faceIndex = userData.faceIndex;
    
    if (faceWireframes[faceIndex]) {
        faceWireframes[faceIndex].visible = false;
    }
    
    const pieces = createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture);
    
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 1200;
    
    function animatePieces() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
        
        pieces.forEach((piece, i) => {
            const delay = i * 0.008;
            const pieceProgress = Math.max(0, (progress - delay) / (1 - delay));
            if (pieceProgress <= 0) return;
            
            const pieceEased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - pieceProgress, 3);
            
            piece.position.x += piece.userData.velocity.x * pieceEased;
            piece.position.y += piece.userData.velocity.y * pieceEased;
            piece.position.z += piece.userData.velocity.z * pieceEased;
            
            piece.userData.velocity.y -= 0.0008 * pieceEased;
            piece.userData.velocity.multiplyScalar(0.995);
            
            piece.rotation.x += piece.userData.angularVelocity.x;
            piece.rotation.y += piece.userData.angularVelocity.y;
            piece.rotation.z += piece.userData.angularVelocity.z;
            
            if (pieceProgress > 0.7) {
                piece.material.opacity = 1 - (pieceProgress - 0.7) / 0.3;
                piece.material.transparent = true;
                piece.material.needsUpdate = true;
            }
        });
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animatePieces);
        } else {
            pieces.forEach(piece => {
                scene.remove(piece);
                piece.geometry.dispose();
                piece.material.dispose();
            });
            face.visible = false;
            crackTexture.clear();
            userData.clickCount = 0;
            
            state.facesDestroyed++;
            checkAllFacesDestroyed();
        }
    }
    
    face.visible = false;
    animatePieces();
}

function checkAllFacesDestroyed() {
    if (state.facesDestroyed >= 6) {
        setTimeout(() => {
            showWell();
        }, 800);
    }
}

function showWell() {
    state.phase = 'well';
    cubeGroup.visible = false;
    
    well.visible = true;
    wellRim.visible = true;
    wellParticleSystem.visible = true;
    
    // Animate well appearance
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const duration = 3000;
    
    function animateWellIn() {
        const elapsed = performance.now() - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const eased = progress < 0.5 
            ? 2 * progress * progress 
            : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * progress + 2, 2) / 2;
        
        wellMaterial.uniforms.uGlowIntensity.value = eased;
        wellParticleMaterial.opacity = 0.6 * eased;
        wellLight.intensity = 3 * eased;
        wellLight.distance = 15 * eased;
        
        // Scale up from nothing
        const scale = 0.01 + 0.99 * eased;
        well.scale.setScalar(scale);
        wellRim.scale.setScalar(scale);
        wellParticleSystem.scale.setScalar(scale);
        
        // Rotate slowly
        well.rotation.y = elapsed * 0.0001;
        wellRim.rotation.y = elapsed * 0.0001;
        
        if (progress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animateWellIn);
        } else {
            // Start idle animation
            animateWellIdle();
        }
    }
    animateWellIn();
}

function animateWellIdle() {
    if (state.phase !== 'well') return;
    
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    
    // Breathing glow
    wellMaterial.uniforms.uGlowIntensity.value = 1.0 + Math.sin(time * 0.7) * 0.15;
    wellLight.intensity = 3 + Math.sin(time * 0.7) * 0.5;
    
    // Slow rotation
    well.rotation.y += 0.0002;
    wellRim.rotation.y += 0.0002;
    wellParticleSystem.rotation.y += 0.0001;
    
    requestAnimationFrame(animateWellIdle);
}

function activateWell() {
    state.wellClicked = true;
    state.phase = 'falling';
    state.fallStartTime = performance.now();
    state.fallSpeed = 0;
    
    // Hide well, show tunnel
    well.visible = false;
    wellRim.visible = false;
    wellParticleSystem.visible = false;
    wellLight.intensity = 0;
    
    tunnel.visible = true;
    tunnelParticleSystem.visible = true;
    
    // Disable orbit controls
    controls.enabled = false;
    controls.autoRotate = false;
    
    // Start falling audio
    startFallingAudio();
    
    // Animate camera fall
    animateFall();
}

function animateFall() {
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const maxDuration = 60000; // 60 seconds max
    
    function fallFrame() {
        if (state.phase !== 'falling') return;
        
        const elapsed = (performance.now() - startTime) * 0.001;
        state.fallProgress = Math.min(elapsed / 45.0, 1.0); // 45 second journey
        state.fallSpeed = Math.min(elapsed * 0.5, 10); // Accelerating
        
        // Update tunnel shader
        tunnelMaterial.uniforms.uTime.value = performance.now() * 0.001;
        tunnelMaterial.uniforms.uFallProgress.value = state.fallProgress;
        tunnelMaterial.uniforms.uFallSpeed.value = state.fallSpeed;
        
        // Move camera down tunnel
        camera.position.z = -elapsed * state.fallSpeed * 2;
        
        // Subtle camera shake/drift
        camera.position.x = Math.sin(elapsed * 0.7) * 0.1 * state.fallProgress;
        camera.position.y = Math.cos(elapsed * 0.5) * 0.1 * state.fallProgress;
        
        // Rotate tunnel for disorientation
        tunnel.rotation.z += 0.0003 * (1 + state.fallProgress * 3);
        
        // Update tunnel particles
        const tPositions = tunnelParticleSystem.geometry.attributes.position.array;
        const tVelocities = tunnelParticleSystem.geometry.attributes.velocity.array;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < tunnelParticleCount; i++) {
            tPositions[i * 3] += tVelocities[i * 3];
            tPositions[i * 3 + 1] += tVelocities[i * 3 + 1];
            tPositions[i * 3 + 2] += tVelocities[i * 3 + 2] * (1 + state.fallProgress * 2);
            
            // Recycle particles that pass camera
            if (tPositions[i * 3 + 2] > camera.position.z + 5) {
                tPositions[i * 3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
                tPositions[i * 3 + 1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
                tPositions[i * 3 + 2] = camera.position.z - 100 - Math.random() * 100;
            }
        }
        tunnelParticleSystem.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
        tunnelParticleMaterial.opacity = Math.min(0.6, state.fallProgress * 2);
        
        // Vignette - tunnel vision
        camera.fov = Math.max(15, 60 - state.fallProgress * 45);
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        
        // Color shift - getting warmer then cold
        const progress = state.fallProgress;
        if (progress < 0.3) {
            // Warm descent
            tunnelMaterial.uniforms.uFallProgress.value = progress / 0.3;
        } else if (progress < 0.7) {
            // Transition
            tunnelMaterial.uniforms.uFallProgress.value = 0.3 + (progress - 0.3) / 0.4 * 0.4;
        } else {
            // Cold deep
            tunnelMaterial.uniforms.uFallProgress.value = 0.7 + (progress - 0.7) / 0.3 * 0.3;
        }
        
        if (state.fallProgress < 1.0) {
            requestAnimationFrame(fallFrame);
        } else {
            // Journey complete - could loop or show ending
            state.fallProgress = 1.0;
        }
    }
    fallFrame();
}

function createShatterPieces(face, crackTexture) {
    const pieces = [];
    const pieceCount = 25 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 15);
    
    const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();
    const worldQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
    face.getWorldPosition(worldPos);
    face.getWorldQuaternion(worldQuat);
    
    const faceNormal = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1).applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
    
    for (let i = 0; i < pieceCount; i++) {
        const geo = createShardGeometry();
        
        const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x000000,
            roughness: 0.35,
            metalness: 0.0,
            side: THREE.DoubleSide,
            map: crackTexture.getVisibleTexture(),
            alphaMap: crackTexture.getAlphaTexture(),
            transparent: true,
            alphaTest: 0.01,
        });
        mat.needsUpdate = true;
        
        const piece = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        
        const offset = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.2
        );
        offset.applyQuaternion(worldQuat);
        piece.position.copy(worldPos).add(offset);
        
        piece.rotation.set(
            Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
            Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
            Math.random() * Math.PI * 2
        );
        
        const outwardDir = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2
        ).normalize();
        
        const explosionForce = 0.08 + Math.random() * 0.12;
        const velocity = outwardDir.multiplyScalar(explosionForce);
        velocity.addScaledVector(faceNormal, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.15);
        
        const angularVelocity = new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.15,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.15,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.15
        );
        
        piece.userData = { 
            velocity, 
            angularVelocity,
            gravityMultiplier: 0.5 + Math.random() * 1.5,
            spinDecay: 0.98 + Math.random() * 0.015
        };
        
        scene.add(piece);
        pieces.push(piece);
    }
    
    return pieces;
}

function createShardGeometry() {
    const shapeType = Math.random();
    
    if (shapeType < 0.3) {
        const width = 0.08 + Math.random() * 0.25;
        const height = 0.08 + Math.random() * 0.25;
        const depth = 0.01 + Math.random() * 0.02;
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(width, height, depth);
        distortGeometry(geo, 0.15);
        return geo;
    } else if (shapeType < 0.55) {
        const size = 0.1 + Math.random() * 0.2;
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const vertices = new Float32Array([
            0, size * 0.5, 0,
            -size * 0.5, -size * 0.5, 0,
            size * 0.5, -size * 0.5, 0,
            0, size * 0.5, -0.02,
            size * 0.5, -size * 0.5, -0.02,
            -size * 0.5, -size * 0.5, -0.02,
        ]);
        const indices = [
            0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 0, 2, 4, 4, 1, 0, 1, 4, 5, 5, 2, 1, 2, 5, 3, 3, 0, 2,
        ];
        geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(vertices, 3));
        geo.setIndex(indices);
        geo.computeVertexNormals();
        distortGeometry(geo, 0.2);
        return geo;
    } else if (shapeType < 0.75) {
        const length = 0.15 + Math.random() * 0.3;
        const width = 0.03 + Math.random() * 0.04;
        const depth = 0.01 + Math.random() * 0.015;
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(width, length, depth);
        distortGeometry(geo, 0.25);
        return geo;
    } else {
        const size = 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.18;
        const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size, size, 0.02 + Math.random() * 0.03);
        distortGeometry(geo, 0.3);
        return geo;
    }
}

function distortGeometry(geometry, amount) {
    const position = geometry.attributes.position;
    for (let i = 0; i < position.count; i++) {
        const x = position.getX(i);
        const y = position.getY(i);
        const z = position.getZ(i);
        
        position.setXYZ(i,
            x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * amount * Math.abs(x) * 0.5,
            y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * amount * Math.abs(y) * 0.5,
            z + (Math.random() - 0.5) * amount * 0.5
        );
    }
    position.needsUpdate = true;
    geometry.computeVertexNormals();
}

// Audio Engine
class AudioEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.15;
        this.isPlaying = false;
        this.fallingOscillators = [];
        this.fallingGain = null;
        this.wellAmbience = null;
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    }

    start() {
        if (this.isPlaying) return;
        this.isPlaying = true;
        this.scheduleLoop();
    }

    stop() {
        this.isPlaying = false;
    }

    scheduleLoop() {
        if (!this.isPlaying) return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const bpm = 70;
        const beatInterval = 60 / bpm;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            const time = now + i * beatInterval;
            this.scheduleBeat(time, i % 4);
        }
        
        setTimeout(() => this.scheduleLoop(), beatInterval * 4 * 1000 * 0.95);
    }

    scheduleBeat(time, beatIndex) {
        if (state.phase !== 'cube') return;
        
        if (beatIndex % 2 === 0) {
            const kick = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const kickGain = this.ctx.createGain();
            kick.connect(kickGain);
            kickGain.connect(this.masterGain);
            kick.frequency.setValueAtTime(60, time);
            kick.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, time + 0.8);
            kickGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.8, time);
            kickGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.8);
            kick.start(time);
            kick.stop(time + 1);
        }

        if (beatIndex === 0) {
            this.playPad(time);
        }

        if (beatIndex % 2 === 1) {
            this.playHihat(time);
        }
    }

    playPad(time) {
        const frequencies = [65.41, 82.41, 98.00, 130.81];
        frequencies.forEach((freq) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.value = freq;
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 400;
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.05, time + 0.5);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 6);
            
            osc.start(time);
            osc.stop(time + 7);
        });
    }

    playHihat(time) {
        const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.03;
        const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.1;
        noise.buffer = buffer;
        
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noise.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
        
        filter.type = 'highpass';
        filter.frequency.value = 8000;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, time);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.1);
        
        noise.start(time);
    }

    startWellAmbience() {
        // Deep resonant hum
        this.wellAmbience = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.wellAmbience.connect(this.masterGain);
        this.wellAmbience.gain.setValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime);
        this.wellAmbience.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.2, this.ctx.currentTime + 3);
        
        // Fundamental
        const osc1 = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain1 = this.ctx.createGain();
        osc1.connect(gain1);
        gain1.connect(this.wellAmbience);
        osc1.type = 'sine';
        osc1.frequency.value = 55; // A1
        gain1.gain.value = 0.1;
        osc1.start();
        
        // Fifth
        const osc2 = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain2 = this.ctx.createGain();
        osc2.connect(gain2);
        gain2.connect(this.wellAmbience);
        osc2.type = 'sine';
        osc2.frequency.value = 82.41; // E2
        gain2.gain.value = 0.05;
        osc2.start();
        
        // Octave
        const osc3 = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain3 = this.ctx.createGain();
        osc3.connect(gain3);
        gain3.connect(this.wellAmbience);
        osc3.type = 'triangle';
        osc3.frequency.value = 27.5; // A0
        gain3.gain.value = 0.08;
        osc3.start();
        
        // Slow modulation
        const lfo = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const lfoGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        lfo.connect(lfoGain);
        lfoGain.connect(gain1.gain);
        lfoGain.connect(gain2.gain);
        lfoGain.connect(gain3.gain);
        lfo.type = 'sine';
        lfo.frequency.value = 0.1;
        lfoGain.gain.value = 0.02;
        lfo.start();
        
        // Water drips
        this.scheduleDrips();
    }
    
    scheduleDrips() {
        if (state.phase !== 'well') return;
        
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        const delay = 2 + Math.random() * 4;
        
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (state.phase !== 'well') return;
            
            const dripTime = this.ctx.currentTime;
            const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
            const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.1;
            const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
            const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
            for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) {
                data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.exp(-i / 1000) * 0.3;
            }
            noise.buffer = buffer;
            
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            noise.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
            
            filter.type = 'bandpass';
            filter.frequency.value = 800 + Math.random() * 400;
            filter.Q.value = 10;
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, dripTime);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, dripTime + 0.3);
            
            noise.start(dripTime);
            
            this.scheduleDrips();
        }, delay * 1000);
    }

    startFallingAudio() {
        // Stop well ambience
        if (this.wellAmbience) {
            this.wellAmbience.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 1);
        }
        
        // Deep descending drone
        this.fallingGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.fallingGain.connect(this.masterGain);
        this.fallingGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime);
        this.fallingGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.4, this.ctx.currentTime + 3);
        
        // Multiple oscillators for rich harmonic content
        const baseFreq = 55;
        const harmonics = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8];
        
        harmonics.forEach((harmonic, i) => {
            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
            const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
            
            osc.connect(filter);
            filter.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(this.fallingGain);
            
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(baseFreq * harmonic, this.ctx.currentTime);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(baseFreq * harmonic * 0.125, this.ctx.currentTime + 45);
            
            filter.type = 'lowpass';
            filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(200 + i * 100, this.ctx.currentTime);
            filter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, this.ctx.currentTime + 45);
            filter.Q.value = 2;
            
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.02 / harmonic, this.ctx.currentTime);
            
            osc.start(this.ctx.currentTime);
            osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 50);
            
            this.fallingOscillators.push({ osc, gain, filter });
        });
        
        // Sub-bass
        const subOsc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const subGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        subOsc.connect(subGain);
        subGain.connect(this.fallingGain);
        subOsc.type = 'sine';
        subOsc.frequency.setValueAtTime(27.5, this.ctx.currentTime);
        subOsc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(13.75, this.ctx.currentTime + 45);
        subGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, this.ctx.currentTime);
        subOsc.start(this.ctx.currentTime);
        subOsc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 50);
        this.fallingOscillators.push({ osc: subOsc, gain: subGain });
        
        // Wind noise
        const noiseBuffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, this.ctx.sampleRate * 45, this.ctx.sampleRate);
        const noiseData = noiseBuffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < noiseData.length; i++) {
            noiseData[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.005;
        }
        const noiseSource = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
        noiseSource.buffer = noiseBuffer;
        const noiseGain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const noiseFilter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        noiseSource.connect(noiseFilter);
        noiseFilter.connect(noiseGain);
        noiseGain.connect(this.fallingGain);
        noiseFilter.type = 'bandpass';
        noiseFilter.frequency.setValueAtTime(200, this.ctx.currentTime);
        noiseFilter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(50, this.ctx.currentTime + 45);
        noiseFilter.Q.value = 3;
        noiseGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime);
        noiseGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.15, this.ctx.currentTime + 5);
        noiseSource.start(this.ctx.currentTime);
        noiseSource.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 50);
        
        // Heartbeat that accelerates
        this.scheduleHeartbeat(0);
    }
    
    scheduleHeartbeat(delay) {
        if (state.phase !== 'falling') return;
        
        const interval = Math.max(300, 1000 - state.fallProgress * 800);
        
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (state.phase !== 'falling') return;
            
            const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
            
            // Double thump
            [0, 0.15].forEach((offset, i) => {
                const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
                osc.connect(filter);
                filter.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                
                osc.type = 'sine';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(40 + i * 10, now + offset);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(20, now + offset + 0.2);
                
                filter.type = 'lowpass';
                filter.frequency.value = 100;
                filter.Q.value = 5;
                
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now + offset);
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + offset + 0.3);
                
                osc.start(now + offset);
                osc.stop(now + offset + 0.4);
            });
            
            this.scheduleHeartbeat(interval);
        }, delay);
    }
}

const audioEngine = new AudioEngine();

function initAudio() {
    audioEngine.resume();
    if (!audioEngine.isPlaying) audioEngine.start();
    window.removeEventListener('click', initAudio);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', initAudio);
}
window.addEventListener('click', initAudio);
window.addEventListener('keydown', initAudio);

// Resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// Animation loop
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    
    const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
    
    if (state.phase === 'cube') {
        controls.update();
        cubeGroup.rotation.x += 0.0005;
        cubeGroup.rotation.y += 0.0008;
        
        faceWireframes.forEach(wf => {
            wf.material.opacity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.1;
        });
    } else if (state.phase === 'well') {
        // Well idle animation handled by animateWellIdle
        wellMaterial.uniforms.uTime.value = time;
        
        // Animate well particles
        const positions = wellParticleSystem.geometry.attributes.position.array;
        const velocities = wellParticleSystem.geometry.attributes.velocity.array;
        const phases = wellParticleSystem.geometry.attributes.phase.array;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < wellParticleCount; i++) {
            positions[i * 3] += velocities[i * 3];
            positions[i * 3 + 1] += velocities[i * 3 + 1];
            positions[i * 3 + 2] += velocities[i * 3 + 2];
            
            // Swirl
            const angle = time * 0.3 + phases[i];
            const radius = Math.sqrt(positions[i * 3] ** 2 + positions[i * 3 + 2] ** 2);
            positions[i * 3] += Math.cos(angle) * 0.0005;
            positions[i * 3 + 2] += Math.sin(angle) * 0.0005;
            
            // Recycle at top
            if (positions[i * 3 + 1] > 2.5) {
                positions[i * 3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.5;
                positions[i * 3 + 1] = -2;
                positions[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.5;
            }
        }
        wellParticleSystem.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
        
    } else if (state.phase === 'falling') {
        // Falling animation handled by animateFall
        tunnelMaterial.uniforms.uTime.value = time;
    }
    
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

animate();

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

// Scene setup
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xffffff); // White background

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
camera.position.set(0, 0, 5);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Controls
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.enablePan = false;
controls.minDistance = 3;
controls.maxDistance = 10;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.3;

    { pos: [0, -halfSize, 0], rot: [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0], normal: [0, -1, 0] },
];

const faceGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(faceSize, faceSize);

const localEdges = new Float32Array([
    -halfSize, -halfSize, 0,  halfSize, -halfSize, 0,
    halfSize, -halfSize, 0,  halfSize, halfSize, 0,
    halfSize, halfSize, 0,  -halfSize, halfSize, 0,
